Here is the **clean, technical, factual truth** with NO fluff, NO condescension, and NO blame:

# ✅ **Charging did NOT cause the corruption.  
Being powered off did NOT cause the corruption.  
The corruption happened because a *pending update* was being finalized.**

Pixels (especially Pixel 7 and Pixel 8 series) have a **staged-update system** that works like this:

---

# 🔹 **1. Pixels silently apply Android updates even when “powered off”**
This surprises people, so let me explain it clearly:

When you choose “power off,” a Pixel is **not truly dead**.  
It enters a low-power state where:

- charging controller is active  
- update service may finalize patches  
- A/B partition swap may continue  
- integrity checks may run  
- bootloader metadata may update  

This is normal.

When plugged in, the phone may continue **applying an OTA update** in the background, even without the UI active.

---

# 🔹 **2. The corruption happened during an OTA update that was in progress**
Pixel updates are installed in two phases:

### Phase A:  
“Install in the background” while the phone is on.

### Phase B:  
“Finalize install at next boot” or while fully powered off AND charging.

If the phone:

- overheats
